Rule 1142:
If grade value ?d AND type LevelOfEvidence, then grade dimension LEVEL OF EVIDENCE.

Formal Rule:
Head: ?a  gradeDimension  LEVEL_OF_EVIDENCE
Body: ?a  hasGradeValue  ?d  ?d  type  LevelOfEvidence
